# Refusal codes

Every refusal the package can raise is one of these codes. A test walks the syntax tree of every module of
the package: it finds every class that derives from the refusal type, at any depth, and every place one is
made, and it follows a code a place takes from a parameter to every caller (by name, through the module, as
a method, through a partial application). A place the walk cannot follow fails the test unless it is listed
with the values it can give. The walk proves that this table and the table in the code (`errors.REFUSALS`)
are the same set, both ways, with the same next move, and that no place names another move.

Who can act decides the next move:

- `fix`: the call itself is wrong, and a corrected call succeeds on the forest as it is. Change what the
  message names and call again; the same call gets the same refusal.
- `retry`: the same call succeeds later without anyone doing anything. Call again after `after_seconds`,
  and report once the sum of each answer's `waited_seconds` and `after_seconds` reaches
  `stop_after_seconds`.
- `run`: a command this caller may run now, which changes the state. The refusal carries it in `command`,
  from the place that knows the real arguments: a command of this table names no folder, and the commands
  of the older index (`memory-forest index …`) name the forest the call named.
- `report`: only the owner or another account can change what is wrong (a file, a setting, an access
  right). Tell the owner the message. When the cause is a folder this account cannot write, the message
  names what an account that can write it would run.

`tests/test_v1_moves_by_doing.py` proves `fix` and `run` by doing: for every `fix` code a wrong call is
refused with it and changes nothing, and the corrected call then succeeds on the same forest; for every
`run` code the state is made, the command is parsed by the command line's own parser and run, and the
same call then answers. A code with neither kind of row is `retry` or `report`.

A place that names no next move gets its code's move from this table. `index_protocol` is internal: it is
how the index asks itself to try a read again, it is never sent, and it is not in this table.

The "When" column is generated from the code (`python3 scripts/refusals_table.py`), and a test proves the
document equal to it: every message the code's places can give, with `…` where a message names a value;
for a code of the state of the derived folder, every sentence of the claims it can carry.

| `archive_copy_unreadable` | `report` |  | The archive copy cannot be read. |
| `archive_damaged` | `report` |  | A listed record history block is cut short; nothing was written. |
| `archive_not_found` | `fix` |  | A pointer line names an archive copy the manifest does not list.<br>The page is not in the archive manifest. |
| `archive_page_changed` | `retry` |  | The page changed while it was archived; it stays in the forest. |
| `archive_path_invalid` | `fix` |  | A successor is a forest page.<br>Only pages are archived.<br>Only pages of the four forest layers are archived. |
| `archive_proof_failed` | `report` |  | Some lines are not in the page's archive copy.<br>The archive entry belongs to another page.<br>The page has no archive copy to prove against.<br>The record history file does not hold the moved lines byte for byte, in order, after its earlier bytes; nothing was written. |
| `archive_readback_failed` | `report` |  | The archive copy did not read back to the page's SHA-256; nothing was archived. |
| `archive_reason_missing` | `fix` |  | An archived page needs a reason. |

| `config_mismatch` | `report` |  | The forest configuration contains unsupported fields.<br>The forest configuration does not match this schema. |
| `context_source_invalid` | `report` |  | The Structured context source has an invalid trail collection. |
| `context_too_large` | `report` |  | The selected Structured context exceeds its document bound. |
| `credential_candidate_invalid` | `fix` |  | A candidate stands on a forest page.<br>Name a candidate as PATH:LINE:PLACE.<br>Name at least one candidate.<br>The line has no candidate at that place.<br>The page has no such line. |
| `credential_key_exposed` | `report` |  | The ledger key must be readable by its owner only (mode 0600). |
| `credential_key_invalid` | `report` |  | The ledger key is not the size it was made. |
| `credential_key_missing` | `report` |  | The ledger holds judgements but its key is gone; the values it names can no longer be recognised. |
| `credential_ledger_unsafe` | `report` |  | A ledger file must be a regular file, never a link. |
| `credentials_archive_mismatch` | `report` |  | The archive copy does not hold the page as it was; not written. |
| `credentials_fix_incomplete` | `report` |  | A sign-in value would still stand; not written. |
| `credentials_path_invalid` | `fix` |  | Only pages of the four forest layers are masked. |
| `daily_provenance_mismatch` | `fix` |  | Promotion provenance does not exactly bind the selected Daily commits.<br>Structured provenance does not exactly bind the disposed Daily commits. |

nothing of it written.<br>A page did not read back as written.<br>A page of the new cut exists and is not a page of this stream.<br>A page the cut would make already exists.<br>A plan file is a list of operations (or an object with `operations`).<br>A removal would change more than the repeated lines.<br>A removed page is still there.<br>A standing line is `said — "<words>" (YYYY-MM-DD)` or `own — "<words>"`.<br>A standing line keeps the subject a leaf whatever its days.<br>A stream page holds only its title, a description, Timeline, Links and Update record; anything else is cut by sub-topic.<br>A stream page's Timeline holds only dated lines.<br>A stream's pages are leaves.<br>A stream's pages share one folder.<br>A subject is not merged into itself.<br>A subject of several leaves is merged into one leaf before it is demoted.<br>A subject or entry already has this id.<br>A subject's board of this name already exists.<br>A tree does not fold into itself.<br>A tree is folded first (`fold-tree`), then merged.<br>A tree of the fold has no page or index.<br>A tree of this id already exists.<br>A tree's id names its pages and folders; fold it or keep its id.<br>An agent folded into a branch is no tree any more and takes another type (--type).<br>An archived page needs a reason.<br>An area tree is retired into the page that took its rules (--into).<br>An entry of this name is already on the collection.<br>An expected folder is a link.<br>An expected folder is not a folder.<br>An operation's arguments do not fit it.<br>Another subject already has this id.<br>Another subject or entry already has this id.<br>Another subject or entry already holds one of these names.<br>Another subject or entry already holds this name.<br>Both the category and the shared branch keep a collection.<br>Each decision names `subject`, `digest`, `home` and `reason`.<br>Each operation is an object with an `op` and its arguments.<br>Give a new id, a new name, or both.<br>Leaf names of the folded tree would clash in its new folder.<br>Leaves of the category have names the shared branch already holds.<br>Leaves, collection pages and emptied boards are archived this way.<br>Merge the leaf subject into the branch one, or promote it first.<br>Name the pages of one stream.<br>No collection page has this entry.<br>No leaf of this subject was found.<br>No registry line has this id.<br>Once the period is taken off, the pages of one stream share one name.<br>Only a leaf moves this way (a collection page is cut with `forest collections`).<br>Only a leaf subject folds into its category's collection.<br>Only a leaf's frontmatter is set this way.<br>Only a subject whose type is in levels.tree_types becomes a tree.<br>Only a tree with its own registry line (level tree) folds into a branch.<br>Only an agent's tree is filled from its settings entry.<br>Only pages of the four forest layers are written.<br>Pages of a tree of this id already exist.<br>The agent has its tree already.<br>The agent is retired already.<br>The archive copy does not hold the page; not written.<br>The branch already has that name.<br>The branch holds pages of other subjects; move them first.<br>The branch is already there.<br>The collection's id is held by another subject.<br>The collection's new place gives it an id another subject holds.<br>The collections tool has no such action.<br>The decisions file cannot be read.<br>The decisions file is not readable JSON.<br>The entry's id cannot name a leaf.<br>The entry's id is already a registered subject.<br>The index leaf keeps its reference lines in no section.<br>The kind is not one of the contract's leaf kinds.<br>The move tool has no such operation.<br>The other tree already has a branch of that name.<br>The other tree already has a branch of that name; | `forest_identity_mismatch` | `fix` |  | The Daily plan is bound to a different Memory Forest.<br>The Structured sweep plan is bound to a different Memory Forest.<br>The promotion plan is bound to a different Memory Forest. |
| `forest_identity_missing` | `report` |  | This forest predates write identities; canonical writes require a forest_id created by a supported migration or new initialization. |
| `forest_invalid` | `report` |  | Indexing requires a forest that passes validation and audit. |
| `fts5_unavailable` | `report` |  | The local SQLite FTS5 index could not be built. |
| `group_full` | `report` |  | `…` already has its max_members (…). The owner raises max_members in forest.toml. … |
| `group_unknown` | `report` |  | There is no group `…`. The owner declares it in forest.toml. … |
| `hook_incomplete` | `fix` |  | The hook payload has no session id.<br>The hook payload has no transcript path. |
| `hook_invalid` | `fix` |  | The hook payload is not a JSON object. |
| `hook_timeout` | `retry` |  | The hook payload did not arrive. |
| `hook_too_large` | `fix` |  | The hook payload is larger than intake reads. |

| `invalid_arguments` | `fix` |  | Name the pages, or pass --all for every page.<br>Pass exactly one of --sign-in and --not-sign-in.<br>The command arguments are invalid. |
| `invalid_config` | `report` |  | The forest configuration is not valid JSON. |
| `invalid_daily_date` | `fix` |  | Daily filenames must use a valid YYYY-MM-DD date. |
| `invalid_daily_machine_block` | `report` |  | A Daily entry marker does not match its payload identifier.<br>A Daily entry marker exists outside a valid provenance-bound block.<br>A Daily entry marker is not bound to its payload and provenance.<br>A Daily entry payload violates the v1 bounds.<br>A Daily machine block batch does not match its transaction.<br>A Daily machine block contains invalid provenance hashes.<br>A Daily machine block is missing exact provenance.<br>A Daily machine payload has missing or unsupported fields.<br>A Daily machine payload is not strict JSON.<br>Daily entry markers must use the exact machine block shape. |
| `invalid_daily_path` | `fix` |  | Daily sources must be root-level ISO-date Markdown files. |
| `invalid_daily_plan` | `fix` |  | A Structured sweep may change each semantic target only once.<br>A create change must use a null expected_sha256.<br>A plan object has missing or unsupported fields.<br>A promoted disposition must name at least one change in this sweep.<br>A promotion plan may target each semantic route only once.<br>A source_only disposition may not name a structured target.<br>Daily entry identifiers must be unique.<br>Every source-bound change must match its exact promoted disposition target.<br>One disposition may not repeat a semantic target.<br>Shared semantic parents must use one exact display title.<br>Structured changes support only create or replace.<br>Structured disposition status is unsupported.<br>Structured dispositions must use unique Daily entry identifiers.<br>This disposition status requires at least one semantic target.<br>Unsupported Daily plan schema.<br>Unsupported Structured sweep plan schema.<br>Unsupported promotion plan schema.<br>confidence must be exactly low, medium, or high.<br>daily_commit_sha256s must contain sorted unique hashes.<br>date must be a valid ISO calendar date.<br>date must be an ISO YYYY-MM-DD string.<br>date must use exact YYYY-MM-DD form.<br>forest_id must be exactly 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters.<br>provenance.batch_id must equal transaction_id.<br>provenance.result_sha256 must equal transaction_id.<br>… contains a forbidden control.<br>… contains an invalid surrogate.<br>… does not match its layer object shape.<br>… must be a bounded portable identifier.<br>… must be a lowercase ASCII semantic slug.<br>… must be a single line.<br>… must be exactly 64 lowercase hexadecimal characters.<br>… must be non-empty and at most … characters.<br>… must contain between … and … items.<br>… must contain unique identifiers.<br>… must use NFC Unicode.<br>….layer must be xltm, ltm, mtm, or stm. |
| `invalid_document_path` | `fix` |  | A document path must include a layer directory and memory file.<br>Document paths must use forward slashes. |
| `invalid_istm_path` | `fix` |  | A raw lane is `06 istm/<file>.jsonl` or `06 istm/<agent>/<file>.jsonl`. |
| `invalid_jsonl` | `report` |  | An ISTM event line is not valid JSON. |
| `invalid_jsonl_event` | `report` |  | Each ISTM event line must be a JSON object. |
| `invalid_life_archive_path` | `fix` |  | Life Archive records may use at most domain/branch/leaf depth. |
| `invalid_limit` | `fix` |  | Structured context limit must be between 1 and 10.<br>The result limit is outside the permitted range. |
| `invalid_lock_name` | `fix` |  | A lock name is a slug. |
| `invalid_ltm_path` | `fix` |  | LTM domains must use 02 ltm/<domain>_LTM.md. |
| `invalid_mtm_path` | `fix` |  | MTM branches must use 03 mtm/<domain>/<branch>.md. |
| `invalid_path` | `fix` |  | A forest path must be non-empty forward-slash text without control characters.<br>A forest path must use NFC Unicode normalization.<br>A forest path runs through something that is not a directory.<br>A page path is at most … bytes.<br>A page path is forward-slash text without control characters.<br>A part of a page path is at most … bytes.<br>A state path must lie under .forest/.<br>Name a page: the path has no part.<br>That path is not a path this system can name. |
| `invalid_promotion_plan` | `fix` |  | A Structured sweep may change each semantic target only once.<br>A create change must use a null expected_sha256.<br>A plan object has missing or unsupported fields.<br>A promoted disposition must name at least one change in this sweep.<br>A promotion plan may target each semantic route only once.<br>A source_only disposition may not name a structured target.<br>Daily entry identifiers must be unique.<br>Every source-bound change must match its exact promoted disposition target.<br>One disposition may not repeat a semantic target.<br>Shared semantic parents must use one exact display title.<br>Structured changes support only create or replace.<br>Structured disposition status is unsupported.<br>Structured dispositions must use unique Daily entry identifiers.<br>This disposition status requires at least one semantic target.<br>Unsupported Daily plan schema.<br>Unsupported Structured sweep plan schema.<br>Unsupported promotion plan schema.<br>confidence must be exactly low, medium, or high.<br>daily_commit_sha256s must contain sorted unique hashes.<br>date must be a valid ISO calendar date.<br>date must be an ISO YYYY-MM-DD string.<br>date must use exact YYYY-MM-DD form.<br>forest_id must be exactly 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters.<br>provenance.batch_id must equal transaction_id.<br>provenance.result_sha256 must equal transaction_id.<br>… contains a forbidden control.<br>… contains an invalid surrogate.<br>… does not match its layer object shape.<br>… must be a bounded portable identifier.<br>… must be a lowercase ASCII semantic slug.<br>… must be a single line.<br>… must be exactly 64 lowercase hexadecimal characters.<br>… must be non-empty and at most … characters.<br>… must contain between … and … items.<br>… must contain unique identifiers.<br>… must use NFC Unicode.<br>….layer must be xltm, ltm, mtm, or stm. |
| `invalid_query_plan` | `fix` |  | A QueryPlan must contain only schema_version and probes.<br>QueryPlan probes must be a non-empty list within the configured limit.<br>The QueryPlan must be strict JSON with unique object keys.<br>The QueryPlan must be valid UTF-8 JSON. |
| `invalid_query_plan_probe` | `fix` |  | Each QueryPlan probe must contain only a query string.<br>Probe queries must be trimmed NFC text without control characters. |
| `invalid_receipt` | `report` |  | A receipt count must be a nonnegative integer.<br>A receipt verification proof has an invalid shape.<br>A successful receipt must bind ok to true.<br>A successful receipt proof must bind ok to true.<br>A successful receipt proof must contain zero integer errors.<br>Receipt paths must be sorted and unique.<br>The receipt Memory Forest identity is invalid.<br>The receipt date is invalid.<br>The receipt filename does not match its transaction.<br>The receipt index path is not canonical.<br>The receipt index proof has an invalid shape.<br>The receipt operation is unsupported.<br>The receipt plan digest is invalid.<br>The receipt touched list is invalid.<br>The receipt transaction is invalid.<br>The transaction receipt contains unsupported fields.<br>The transaction receipt is not strict UTF-8 JSON.<br>The transaction receipt must be a JSON object.<br>The transaction receipt must use canonical JSON bytes.<br>The transaction receipt schema is unsupported. |
| `invalid_retrieval_config` | `report` |  | The optional retrieval configuration must contain only query_plan.<br>The query_plan configuration must contain only max_probes.<br>query_plan.max_probes must be an integer from 1 through 16. |
| `invalid_route_segment` | `fix` |  | A route segment contains a forbidden character or is too long.<br>Route segments must be non-empty and may not be dot segments. |
| `invalid_stm_path` | `fix` |  | STM leaves must use 04 stm/<domain>/<branch>/<leaf>.md. |

| `link_count_exceeded` | `report` |  | The forest exceeds the configured wikilink limit. |
| `lint_code_invalid` | `fix` |  | A code keeps its class. |
| `lint_code_unknown` | `fix` |  | No such lint code. |
| `lock_already_held` | `fix` |  | This process already holds the lock; pass its token to re-enter it. |
| `lock_busy` | `retry` |  | Another thread of this process is writing the index.<br>Another writer holds the lock. |
| `lock_holder_stopped` | `report` |  | The process that holds the lock is stopped, so waiting does not release it. Continue that process, or end it, and ask again. |
| `lock_release_failed` | `report` |  | The maintenance lock could not be released safely. |
| `lock_token_invalid` | `report` |  | The token passed in names no live hold of this lock. |
| `lock_unsupported` | `report` |  | Forest locks need a POSIX system. |
| `log_agent_unset` | `fix` |  | Name the agent with `forest log --as`, or the group and the caller with `forest log --as-group` and `--agent`. |
| `log_caller_fields` | `fix` |  | `forest log` sets the role and the origin. A caller cannot. Leave out --role and --origin. |
| `log_read_only` | `report` |  | This account cannot write the forest's .forest folder or the agent's raw lane, so the turn was not written. An account that can write the forest keeps it with `forest log --as …`, the turn on stdin. |
| `log_read_timeout` | `retry` |  | `forest log` stopped after … seconds because stdin did not end. The turn was stored nowhere. |
| `log_text_from_stdin` | `fix` |  | The turn is read from stdin, never from an argument. An argument would show in the process list. Write the turn on stdin of `forest log --as <agent>`. |
| `log_too_large` | `fix` |  | The turn is longer than … bytes. It was not stored.<br>The turn is longer than … bytes. `forest log` did not store it. |
| `maintenance_lock_busy` | `retry` |  | The sibling maintenance lock is already held. |
| `mcp_agent_unset` | `fix` |  | Name the agent with --as, or name the group with --as-group. Pass one of them. |
| `mcp_bind` | `fix` |  | --http is PORT or HOST:PORT. |
| `mcp_group_invalid` | `fix` |  | Name the group with --as-group. The group id is a slug. |
| `mcp_reader_conflict` | `fix` |  | Pass --as or --as-group, not both. A tool argument cannot change which one it is. |
| `mcp_token_invalid` | `report` |  | The MCP token file must hold one line. |
| `mcp_token_missing` | `report` |  | The MCP server needs .forest/mcp/token, a regular file in mode 0600. |
| `mcp_token_mode` | `report` |  | The MCP token file must be mode 0600. Its contents were not shown. |
| `member_source_failed` | `report` |  | The source of members `…` of the group `…` …, so it is not known whether this name is a member. The owner looks at that source; nothing was written. |

| `no_forest` | `fix` |  | No forest.toml in …. Name the forest's folder with `--root <folder>`; `forest init <folder>` makes a new forest in an empty folder. |
| `non_regular_file` | `report` |  | A forest page is not a regular file.<br>Memory documents must be regular files.<br>Only regular files and directories are allowed in a forest.<br>… is not a regular file. |
| `noncanonical_layer_directory` | `fix` |  | Layer directories must use the canonical space-separated name. |
| `noncanonical_route_segment` | `fix` |  | Route segments must use NFC Unicode normalization. |
| `noncanonical_write_path` | `report` |  | Structured targets must resolve to exact canonical paths.<br>Writer targets must use exact canonical paths. |

or a backtick).<br>The … must be a slug ([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*).<br>The … must be at most … characters, so it can be a file name.<br>The … must not be empty.<br>The … names no registered subject or collection entry.<br>There is no such board.<br>There is no such page in the forest.<br>There is no such tree.<br>This page has no parent page.<br>This page is removed earlier in the plan.<br>Two leaves would take one name in a branch of the new tree.<br>Two leaves would take one name in the subject's folder; | `query_failed` | `report` |  | The local SQLite FTS5 index could not be queried.<br>The local SQLite FTS5 index could not provide Structured context. |
| `query_plan_changed` | `retry` |  | The QueryPlan source changed while it was being opened. |
| `query_plan_input_unavailable` | `fix` |  | The QueryPlan source '-' requires a binary standard-input stream. |
| `query_plan_not_found` | `fix` |  | The selected QueryPlan file does not exist. |
| `query_plan_open_failed` | `report` |  | The QueryPlan file could not be opened safely. |
| `query_plan_too_large` | `fix` |  | The QueryPlan exceeds the 32768-byte limit. |
| `query_too_long` | `fix` |  | The query exceeds the 1000-character limit. |
| `read_failed` | `report` |  | A file could not be read as text. |
| `read_lines` | `fix` |  | Lines are written A-B, for example 4-12.<br>Lines are written A-B, with A at least 1. |
| `read_lines_empty` | `fix` |  | None of those lines is a turn that came through that source.<br>Those lines fall outside the addressed section. |
| `reader_conflict` | `fix` |  | Pass --as or --as-group on `forest log`, not both.<br>Pass --as or --as-group, not both. |

| `receipt_capacity_exceeded` | `report` |  | The private receipt store is full; archive receipts before writing. |
| `receipt_collision` | `retry` |  | A receipt appeared during the transaction. |
| `receipt_conflict` | `report` |  | The existing receipt does not bind this exact write plan. |
| `receipt_count_exceeded` | `report` |  | The receipts directory exceeds the supported entry limit. |
| `receipt_state_mismatch` | `report` |  | The receipt exists but the Structured canonical state has changed.<br>The receipt exists but the canonical Daily transaction is missing.<br>The receipt exists but the promoted canonical state has changed. |
| `receipt_too_large` | `report` |  | The deterministic receipt exceeds its supported byte limit. |
| `receipt_write_failed` | `report` |  | The transaction receipt could not be created safely. |
| `record_missing` | `fix` |  | The page has no update record. |
| `record_page_changed` | `retry` |  | The page changed while it was read; nothing was written.<br>The page changed while it was trimmed; nothing was written. |
| `record_path_invalid` | `fix` |  | Only pages of the four forest layers have a record to trim. |
| `record_trim_unsafe` | `report` |  | A trim would change the page outside its update record; nothing was written.<br>A trim would lose or add a record line; nothing was written. |
| `registry_invalid` | `run` | `forest registry build` | The registry file is not JSON; rebuild it.<br>The registry file is not a JSON object; rebuild it.<br>The registry file is not a memory-forest registry of this version; rebuild it. |
| `registry_missing` | `run` | `forest registry build` | The forest has no compiled registry; run `forest registry build`. |
| `registry_unreadable` | `report` |  | The registry file is not a regular file; the owner removes what stands in its place. |

| `rollback_failed` | `report` |  | The failed write could not be fully rolled back. |
| `root_above_file` | `fix` |  | A part of the path is a file (…), so the forest folder is not there. |
| `root_exists` | `fix` |  | Initialization requires a new path and never writes into an existing one. |
| `root_not_directory` | `fix` |  | The forest folder is not a folder.<br>The selected forest root is not a directory. |
| `root_not_empty` | `fix` |  | forest init needs a new or empty folder. |
| `root_not_found` | `fix` |  | The selected forest root does not exist.<br>There is nothing at the path named for the forest folder. |
| `root_not_owned` | `report` |  | The selected forest root must be owned by the current user. |
| `scan_failed` | `report` |  | A forest directory could not be scanned. |
| `scope_widened` | `fix` |  | This command is an agent's door, so it takes no flag that chooses a wider scope. Leave out --named. The tools take no such argument. |
| `search_empty` | `fix` |  | Search needs the words to look for. |
| `search_limit` | `fix` |  | The limit is at least 1. |
| `settings_invalid` | `report` |  | forest.toml could not be read (…). The owner mends or replaces forest.toml. Tell the owner what the details name.<br>forest.toml does not hold settings this package can read. The owner mends or replaces forest.toml.<br>forest.toml is a link; the forest's settings are a regular file, never a link.<br>forest.toml is a symlink. The owner mends or replaces forest.toml.<br>forest.toml is a …, not a regular file.<br>forest.toml is larger than any settings file needs to be.<br>forest.toml is not UTF-8 text.<br>forest.toml is not a regular file.<br>forest.toml is not a regular file. | `settings_unpatched` | `report` |  | forest.toml could not be safely patched or its settings lock could not be acquired, so nothing was written and the call did not run. The owner makes these edits: … |
| `settings_unwritable` | `report` |  | forest.toml could not be written, so nothing was written and the call did not run. The owner makes these edits: … |
| `source_unknown` | `fix` |  | `…` is not a source of the turns this reader may read. A source is written `group:<id>`, `door:<name>` or `adapter:<name>`; pass one of the sources listed. |

rename the category branch instead.<br>A current-state line is not where the pass read it.<br>A decision's home is one of the leaves that hold the line.<br>A decisions file is a list of decisions (or an object with `decisions`).<br>A leaf goes to `04 stm/<tree>/<branch>/<leaf>.md`.<br>A leaf of this id already exists.<br>A leaf's sub-topic cannot name a branch.<br>A month page the cut would make already exists.<br>A new tree needs a description of what lives there.<br>A page already exists where this one would go.<br>A page changed on disk while the plan was made.<br>A page changed while the pass read it; nothing of it written.<br>A page did not read back as written.<br>A page of the new cut exists and is not a page of this stream.<br>A page the cut would make already exists.<br>A plan file is a list of operations (or an object with `operations`).<br>A removal would change more than the repeated lines.<br>A removed page is still there.<br>A standing line is `said — "<words>" (YYYY-MM-DD)` or `own — "<words>"`.<br>A standing line keeps the subject a leaf whatever its days.<br>A stream page holds only its title, a description, Timeline, Links and Update record; anything else is cut by sub-topic.<br>A stream page's Timeline holds only dated lines.<br>A stream's pages are leaves.<br>A stream's pages share one folder.<br>A subject is not merged into itself.<br>A subject of several leaves is merged into one leaf before it is demoted.<br>A subject or entry already has this id.<br>A subject's board of this name already exists.<br>A tree does not fold into itself.<br>A tree is folded first (`fold-tree`), then merged.<br>A tree of the fold has no page or index.<br>A tree of this id already exists.<br>A tree's id names its pages and folders; fold it or keep its id.<br>An agent folded into a branch is no tree any more and takes another type (--type).<br>An archived page needs a reason.<br>An area tree is retired into the page that took its rules (--into).<br>An entry of this name is already on the collection.<br>An expected folder is a link.<br>An expected folder is not a folder.<br>An operation's arguments do not fit it.<br>Another subject already has this id.<br>Another subject or entry already has this id.<br>Another subject or entry already holds one of these names.<br>Another subject or entry already holds this name.<br>Both the category and the shared branch keep a collection.<br>Each decision names `subject`, `digest`, `home` and `reason`.<br>Each operation is an object with an `op` and its arguments.<br>Give a new id, a new name, or both.<br>Leaf names of the folded tree would clash in its new folder.<br>Leaves of the category have names the shared branch already holds.<br>Leaves, collection pages and emptied boards are archived this way.<br>Merge the leaf subject into the branch one, or promote it first.<br>Name the pages of one stream.<br>No collection page has this entry.<br>No leaf of this subject was found.<br>No registry line has this id.<br>Once the period is taken off, the pages of one stream share one name.<br>Only a leaf moves this way (a collection page is cut with `forest collections`).<br>Only a leaf subject folds into its category's collection.<br>Only a leaf's frontmatter is set this way.<br>Only a subject whose type is in levels.tree_types becomes a tree.<br>Only a tree with its own registry line (level tree) folds into a branch.<br>Only an agent's tree is filled from its settings entry.<br>Only pages of the four forest layers are written.<br>Pages of a tree of this id already exist.<br>The agent has its tree already.<br>The agent is retired already.<br>The archive copy does not hold the page; | `unsafe_index_path` | `report` |  | The index path contains a symlink or unexpected file type.<br>The index path must be a regular file or not exist. |
| `unsafe_index_staging` | `report` |  | The index staging directory is unavailable.<br>The index staging directory must be a real private sibling on the same filesystem. |
| `unsafe_lock_parent` | `report` |  | The maintenance lock parent must be a real directory. |
| `unsafe_mutation_target` | `report` |  | A mutation target changed to an unsafe file type.<br>A private writer file could not be opened safely.<br>A private writer file exceeds its byte limit.<br>A private writer file has an unsafe type, mode, or size.<br>Canonical write targets must be real private files. |
| `unsafe_parent_directory` | `report` |  | Writer parent directories must be real and private. |
| `unsafe_plan_permissions` | `report` |  | The write plan must be owned by the current user.<br>The write plan must be private, non-executable, and owner-readable. |
| `unsafe_plan_source` | `report` |  | The plan path must have no symlink components.<br>The write plan could not be opened safely.<br>The write plan must be a regular non-symlink file. |
| `unsafe_query_plan_source` | `report` |  | The QueryPlan source must be a regular non-symlink file. |
| `unsafe_receipt_entry` | `report` |  | A private writer file could not be opened safely.<br>A private writer file exceeds its byte limit.<br>A private writer file has an unsafe type, mode, or size.<br>Receipt entries must be bounded private transaction JSON files. |
| `unsafe_receipts_directory` | `report` |  | A required directory must be real and mode 0700.<br>A required private directory is missing.<br>The receipts directory must be a real private directory. |
| `unsafe_state_directory` | `report` |  | The derived-state path must be a real directory. |
| `unsafe_state_entry` | `report` |  | Derived state may contain only known files and receipts. |
| `unsafe_transcript` | `fix` |  | A transcript must be a regular file, never a symlink.<br>A transcript path cannot contain '..'.<br>The transcript is outside the tool's transcript root.<br>The transcript path could not be resolved. |
| `unsafe_write_journal` | `report` |  | A durable write-journal JSON file is invalid.<br>A durable write-journal backup binding is invalid.<br>A durable write-journal backup hash is invalid.<br>A durable write-journal directory is invalid.<br>A durable write-journal path is invalid.<br>A durable write-journal staging file is unsafe.<br>A durable write-journal target is invalid.<br>A private writer file could not be opened safely.<br>A private writer file exceeds its byte limit.<br>A private writer file has an unsafe type, mode, or size.<br>A write-journal phase marker is invalid.<br>An absent write target may not carry a backup.<br>The durable write-journal manifest is invalid.<br>The write journal contains an unsupported entry.<br>The write journal exceeds its entry bound.<br>The write journal has conflicting terminal states.<br>The write journal must be a real private directory. |
| `unsupported_file_type` | `fix` |  | Canonical memory documents must use .md, except ISTM .jsonl streams. |
| `unsupported_layer` | `report` |  | The memory layer is not supported by this schema. |
| `unsupported_query_plan_schema` | `fix` |  | The QueryPlan schema version is unsupported. |
| `write_journal_exists` | `report` |  | An earlier write journal must be recovered before a new transaction. |
